---
title: "2026-08-18 - Developer ports Android 13 to reMarkable Paper Pro, unlocking new possibilities - neowin.net"
source: "Neowin"
author: "[[Sanket Mungase]]"
published: 2026-08-18
created: 2026-08-18
description: "A YouTuber gives the reMarkable Paper Pro an unexpected upgrade by porting Android 13, bringing apps like OneNote and Kindle to the E-Ink tablet."
tags:
  - "clippings"
url: "https://www.neowin.net/news/developer-ports-android-13-to-remarkable-paper-pro-unlocking-new-possibilities/?utm_source=rss"
---
![A person standing outdoors and writing on a reMarkable tablet with a stylus while holding it in one ](https://cdn.neowin.com/news/images/uploaded/2026/08/1787082981_remarkable.webp)

Image via reMarkable

The reMarkable Paper Pro is a popular E-Ink tablet powered by Linux and specifically designed to keep users focused by purposely excluding web browsers, app stores, and multimedia distractions. However, tech enthusiast and creator Chalid Raqami has been able to successfully port an Android operating system, in addition to the Linux operating system, to the digital notebook.

YouTuber Chalid Raqami carried out the experiment, where he managed to get Android 13 running on the Paper Pro. Raqami, rather than completely replacing the factory OS, modified the tablet so that users can easily switch between the original [Linux OS](https://www.neowin.net/news/linux-73-merge-window-kicks-off-by-throwing-out-two-ancient-file-systems/) and a full Android 13 interface in about 30 seconds.

This dual operating system environment allows users to access everyday applications while retaining the tablet’s responsive note-taking feature. Standard Android apps such as Amazon Kindle for color comics, Microsoft OneNote, web browsers, and audiobook players can also be installed.

The biggest challenge for Raqami was the 2GB of RAM, since running newer Android versions for modern app compatibility was difficult due to memory constraints. To overcome this, the YouTuber first tried installing Android 12 OS and, after a breakthrough, proceeded with Android 13.

![](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1KmsHvUbc9k)

Raqami developed a low-latency stylus mode that reduces writing delays, allowing users to write in apps such as Microsoft OneNote and others with minimal lag. Simultaneously, essential hardware features such as Wi-Fi, Bluetooth keyboard, audio connectivity, front-light controls, and sleep cover wake triggers remain fully functional on the tablet.

To improve battery performance, deep sleep was added, reducing standby power consumption to approximately 0.3% per hour and providing hundreds of hours of standby time on a single charge, according to Raqami.

However, there are some trade-offs that users face; due to low RAM and a modest processor, the tablet struggles to run heavy applications such as Obsidian. The Gallery 3 color E-Ink screen prioritizes color richness over speed, making it unsuitable for video playback or rapid scrolling. Also, an update to the original reMarkable software can remove the Android installation.

The custom Android 13 port is not available to the public yet. The YouTuber is going to open testing soon to a smaller group of people to find the remaining issues. If the Android port eventually becomes publicly available and stable enough for everyday use, it could make the Paper Pro a more direct alternative to Android-based E-Ink tablets like the [BOOX Go Gen 2 Lumi](https://www.neowin.net/reviews/boox-go-gen-2-lumi-review-e-ink-android-tablet-with-stunning-hardware-and-rich-software/) and [iFlyTek AINote 2](https://www.neowin.net/handson/hands-on-with-iflytek-ainote-2-e-ink-tablet-insanely-thin-and-smart).

Via [Notebookcheck](https://www.notebookcheck.net/YouTuber-ports-Android-to-reMarkable-Paper-Pro-and-it-brings-huge-benefits.1370419.0.html)

                    <section class="article" data-article-id="267513" data-article-nonce="4458d9ccdbba6808ec684a1c9b24c3ca">
        <div class="wrap">
            <div class="row">

                <article class="article-col d-col-8 w-col-8" id="article-267513">
                    

                    <header class="article-header">
                                                    <span>When you purchase through links on our site, we may earn an affiliate commission. <a href="https://www.neowin.net/terms">Here’s how it works</a>.</span>
                        
                        <h1 class="article-title">
                            <a rel="bookmark" href="https://www.neowin.net/news/developer-ports-android-13-to-remarkable-paper-pro-unlocking-new-possibilities/" title="Permalink to Developer ports Android 13 to reMarkable Paper Pro, unlocking new possibilities">
                            Developer ports Android 13 to reMarkable Paper Pro, unlocking new possibilities                            </a>
                        </h1>
                        <p class="article-subtitle">
                             YouTuber ports Android 13 to the reMarkable Paper Pro, adding third-party apps, better versatility and broader tablet functionality.                        </p>

                        <p class="article-meta">
    <span class="author vcard">
            <a class="fn profile-link" rel="author" href="https://www.neowin.net/profile/724460-sanket-mungase/">Sanket Mungase</a>
        <span class="role"></span>
        <span class="org">Neowin</span>
        <a class="twitter-link" rel="me" href="https://www.twitter.com/SanketM1771">@SanketM1771</a>        ·
    </span>
    <time class="published" datetime="Aug 18, 2026 16:24 EDT" pubdate="pubdate">
    Aug 18, 2026 16:24 EDT
    </time>
            
    <span class="meta-comments">
    <span>with</span> <a href="#comments" class="comment-link scrollto " title="View 0 Comments">0 <span>comments</span></a>
        </span>
    
    </p>

                    </header>

                    <div class="article-content" itemprop="articleBody">
                                                                        <figure class="image image--expandable"><a href="https://cdn.neowin.com/news/images/uploaded/2026/08/1787082981_remarkable.webp"><img alt="A person standing outdoors and writing on a reMarkable tablet with a stylus while holding it in one " height="800" src="https://cdn.neowin.com/news/images/uploaded/2026/08/1787082981_remarkable.webp" width="1200"></a>
<figcaption>Image via <a href="https://remarkable.com/blog/meet-remarkable-paper-pro-move-the-groundbreaking-paper-tablet-made-for-work-on-the-go" target="_blank">reMarkable</a></figcaption>
</figure>

<p>The reMarkable Paper Pro is a popular E-Ink tablet powered by Linux and specifically designed to keep users focused by purposely excluding web browsers, app stores, and multimedia distractions. However, tech enthusiast and creator Chalid Raqami has been able to successfully port an Android operating system, in addition to the Linux operating system, to the digital notebook.</p>

                            <!-- Tag ID: neowin_incontent_all_devices -->
<div align="center" data-freestar-ad="__300x250 __728x250" id="neowin_incontent_all_devices">
  
</div>            <p>YouTuber Chalid Raqami carried out the experiment, where he managed to get Android 13 running on the Paper Pro. Raqami, rather than completely replacing the factory OS, modified the tablet so that users can easily switch between the original <a href="https://www.neowin.net/news/linux-73-merge-window-kicks-off-by-throwing-out-two-ancient-file-systems/" target="_blank">Linux OS</a> and a full Android 13 interface in about 30 seconds.</p>

<p>This dual operating system environment allows users to access everyday applications while retaining the tablet’s responsive note-taking feature. Standard Android apps such as Amazon Kindle for color comics, Microsoft OneNote, web browsers, and audiobook players can also be installed.</p>

<p>The biggest challenge for Raqami was the 2GB of RAM, since running newer Android versions for modern app compatibility was difficult due to memory constraints. To overcome this, the YouTuber first tried installing Android 12 OS and, after a breakthrough, proceeded with Android 13.</p>

<p></p>

<div data-oembed-url="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1KmsHvUbc9k">
<div class="iframely-embed iframely-player iframely-youtube">
<div class="iframely-responsive"><iframe allow="accelerometer *; clipboard-write *; encrypted-media *; gyroscope *; picture-in-picture *; web-share *;" allowfullscreen="" referrerpolicy="strict-origin" scrolling="no" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/1KmsHvUbc9k?rel=0" tabindex="-1"></iframe></div>
</div>
</div>

<p>Raqami developed a low-latency stylus mode that reduces writing delays, allowing users to write in apps such as Microsoft OneNote and others with minimal lag. Simultaneously, essential hardware features such as Wi-Fi, Bluetooth keyboard, audio connectivity, front-light controls, and sleep cover wake triggers remain fully functional on the tablet.</p>

<p>To improve battery performance, deep sleep was added, reducing standby power consumption to approximately 0.3% per hour and providing hundreds of hours of standby time on a single charge, according to Raqami.</p>

<p>However, there are some trade-offs that users face; due to low RAM and a modest processor, the tablet struggles to run heavy applications such as Obsidian. The Gallery 3 color E-Ink screen prioritizes color richness over speed, making it unsuitable for video playback or rapid scrolling. Also, an update to the original reMarkable software can remove the Android installation.</p>

<p>The custom Android 13 port is not available to the public yet. The YouTuber is going to open testing soon to a smaller group of people to find the remaining issues. If the Android port eventually becomes publicly available and stable enough for everyday use, it could make the Paper Pro a more direct alternative to Android-based E-Ink tablets like the <a href="https://www.neowin.net/reviews/boox-go-gen-2-lumi-review-e-ink-android-tablet-with-stunning-hardware-and-rich-software/" target="_blank">BOOX Go Gen 2 Lumi</a> and <a href="https://www.neowin.net/handson/hands-on-with-iflytek-ainote-2-e-ink-tablet-insanely-thin-and-smart" target="_blank">iFlyTek AINote 2</a>.</p>

<p>Via <a href="https://www.notebookcheck.net/YouTuber-ports-Android-to-reMarkable-Paper-Pro-and-it-brings-huge-benefits.1370419.0.html" target="_blank">Notebookcheck</a></p>
                        
                        
                                                                    </div>

                    <div class="article-tags">
    <h4 class="is-hidden">Tags</h4>
